<script type="text/javascript">

    window.addEventListener("load", function () {
      if (window.ga && ga.create) {
        saveAnalyticsClientId();
      }
    }, false);

    function saveAnalyticsClientId() {

      var clientId = getClientIdfromGoogleAnalytics();

      var url = '/api/Customers/SaveAnalyticsSession?clientId=' + clientId;
      $.get(url, function (result) {
        console.log(result);
      });
    }

    function getClientIdfromGoogleAnalytics() {
      if (ga) {
        var trackers = ga.getAll();
        var i, len;
        for (i = 0, len = trackers.length; i < len; i += 1) {
          if (trackers[i].get('trackingId') === 'UA-1736811-1') {
            return trackers[i].get('clientId');
          }
        }
      }
      return "";
    }

  </script>
